-// ****************************************************************************
-// Function: verifyResults
-//
-// Purpose:
-//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution
-//
-// Arguments:
-//   reference: array holding the reference result vector
-//   result: array holding the result vector to verify
-//   size: number of elements per vector
-//
-// ****************************************************************************
-bool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {
+// One thread per row is more efficient for very short rows because it avoids
+// spending an entire warp on only a handful of nonzeros.
+__global__ void spmvScalarKernel(const double* __restrict__ values,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                 const double* __restrict__ vector,
+                                 const index_t rows, double* __restrict__ output) {
+    const index_t row = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row]; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1]; ++item) {
+        sum = fma(values[item], vector[cols[item]], sum);
+    }
+    output[row] = sum;
+}
+
+// A warp cooperatively processes one row. CSR values and column indices are read
+// coalescently, while read-only vector entries benefit from the GPU cache.
+__global__ void spmvWarpKernel(const double* __restrict__ values,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vector,
+                               const index_t rows, double* __restrict__ output) {
+    const index_t globalThread = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const index_t row = globalThread >> 5;
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & 31U;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row] + lane; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+         item += 32) {
+        sum = fma(values[item], vector[cols[item]], sum);
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffU, sum, offset);
+    }
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        output[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(cudaStream_t stream, const double* values, const index_t* cols,
+                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vector, const index_t rows,
+                const index_t nnz, double* output) {
+    if (rows == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const double averageNnz = static_cast<double>(nnz) / rows;
+    if (averageNnz < 12.0) {
+        const int blocks = static_cast<int>((rows + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE);
+        spmvScalarKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
+            values, cols, rowDelimiters, vector, rows, output);
+    } else {
+        constexpr int warpsPerBlock = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / 32;
+        const int blocks = static_cast<int>((rows + warpsPerBlock - 1) / warpsPerBlock);
+        spmvWarpKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
+            values, cols, rowDelimiters, vector, rows, output);
+    }
+}
+
+bool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size,
+                   const index_t globalRowOffset, const int rank) {
     for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {
         const double ref = reference[i];
         const double res = result[i];
-        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {
-            // For very small values, check absolute error
-            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
-                printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\n", i, ref, res);
-                return false;
-            }
-        } else {
-            // Check relative error
-            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);
-            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
-                printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\n",
-                       i, ref, res, relError);
-                return false;
-            }
+        bool invalid = !std::isfinite(ref) || !std::isfinite(res);
+        if (!invalid && std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {
+            invalid = std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR;
+        } else if (!invalid) {
+            invalid = std::abs((res - ref) / ref) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR;
+        }
+        if (invalid) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr,
+                         "Rank %d validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, "
+                         "got %.10e\n",
+                         rank, globalRowOffset + i, ref, res);
+            return false;
         }
     }
     return true;
 }

+void spmvCpu(const double* values, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,
+             const double* vector, const index_t rows, double* output) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (int64_t row = 0; row < static_cast<int64_t>(rows); ++row) {
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row]; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1]; ++item) {
+            sum += values[item] * vector[cols[item]];
         }
-        out[i] = t;
+        output[row] = sum;
     }
 }
 
-// ****************************************************************************
-// Function: verifyResults
-//
-// Purpose:
-//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution
-//
-// Arguments:
-//   reference: array holding the reference result vector
-//   result: array holding the result vector to verify
-//   size: number of elements per vector

-// ****************************************************************************
-bool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {
+// One thread per row is more efficient for very short rows because it avoids
+// spending an entire warp on only a handful of nonzeros.
+__global__ void spmvScalarKernel(const double* __restrict__ values,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                 const double* __restrict__ vector,
+                                 const index_t rows, double* __restrict__ output) {
+    const index_t row = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row]; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1]; ++item) {
+        sum = fma(values[item], vector[cols[item]], sum);
+    }
+    output[row] = sum;
+}
+
+// A warp cooperatively processes one row. CSR values and column indices are read
+// coalescently, while read-only vector entries benefit from the GPU cache.
+__global__ void spmvWarpKernel(const double* __restrict__ values,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vector,
+                               const index_t rows, double* __restrict__ output) {
+    const index_t globalThread = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const index_t row = globalThread >> 5;
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & 31U;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row] + lane; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+         item += 32) {
+        sum = fma(values[item], vector[cols[item]], sum);
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffU, sum, offset);
+    }
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        output[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(cudaStream_t stream, const double* values, const index_t* cols,
+                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vector, const index_t rows,
+                const index_t nnz, double* output) {
+    if (rows == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const double averageNnz = static_cast<double>(nnz) / rows;
+    if (averageNnz < 12.0) {
+        const int blocks = static_cast<int>((rows + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE);
+        spmvScalarKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
+            values, cols, rowDelimiters, vector, rows, output);
+    } else {
+        constexpr int warpsPerBlock = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / 32;
+        const int blocks = static_cast<int>((rows + warpsPerBlock - 1) / warpsPerBlock);
+        spmvWarpKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
+            values, cols, rowDelimiters, vector, rows, output);
+    }
+}
+
+bool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size,
+                   const index_t globalRowOffset, const int rank) {
     for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {
         const double ref = reference[i];
         const double res = result[i];
-        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {
-            // For very small values, check absolute error
-            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
-                printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\n", i, ref, res);
-                return false;
-            }
-        } else {
-            // Check relative error
-            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);
-            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
-                printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\n",
-                       i, ref, res, relError);
-                return false;
-            }
+        bool invalid = !std::isfinite(ref) || !std::isfinite(res);
+        if (!invalid && std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {
+            invalid = std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR;
+        } else if (!invalid) {
+            invalid = std::abs((res - ref) / ref) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR;
+        }
+        if (invalid) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr,
+                         "Rank %d validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, "
+                         "got %.10e\n",
+                         rank, globalRowOffset + i, ref, res);
+            return false;
         }
     }
     return true;
 }

-// ****************************************************************************
-// Function: verifyResults
-//
-// Purpose:
-//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution

-// Arguments:
-//   reference: array holding the reference result vector

-bool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {
+// One thread per row is more efficient for very short rows because it avoids
+// spending an entire warp on only a handful of nonzeros.
+__global__ void spmvScalarKernel(const double* __restrict__ values,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                 const double* __restrict__ vector,
+                                 const index_t rows, double* __restrict__ output) {
+    const index_t row = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row]; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1]; ++item) {
+        sum = fma(values[item], vector[cols[item]], sum);
+    }
+    output[row] = sum;
+}
+
+// A warp cooperatively processes one row. CSR values and column indices are read
+// coalescently, while read-only vector entries benefit from the GPU cache.
+__global__ void spmvWarpKernel(const double* __restrict__ values,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vector,
+                               const index_t rows, double* __restrict__ output) {
+    const index_t globalThread = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const index_t row = globalThread >> 5;
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & 31U;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row] + lane; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+         item += 32) {
+        sum = fma(values[item], vector[cols[item]], sum);
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffU, sum, offset);
+    }
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        output[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(cudaStream_t stream, const double* values, const index_t* cols,
+                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vector, const index_t rows,
+                const index_t nnz, double* output) {
+    if (rows == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const double averageNnz = static_cast<double>(nnz) / rows;
+    if (averageNnz < 12.0) {
+        const int blocks = static_cast<int>((rows + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE);
+        spmvScalarKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
+            values, cols, rowDelimiters, vector, rows, output);
+    } else {
+        constexpr int warpsPerBlock = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / 32;
+        const int blocks = static_cast<int>((rows + warpsPerBlock - 1) / warpsPerBlock);
+        spmvWarpKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
+            values, cols, rowDelimiters, vector, rows, output);
+    }
+}
+
+bool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size,
+                   const index_t globalRowOffset, const int rank) {
     for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {
         const double ref = reference[i];
         const double res = result[i];
-        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {
-            // For very small values, check absolute error
-            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
-                printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\n", i, ref, res);
-                return false;
-            }
-        } else {
-            // Check relative error
-            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);
-            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
-                printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\n",
-                       i, ref, res, relError);
-                return false;
-            }
+        bool invalid = !std::isfinite(ref) || !std::isfinite(res);
+        if (!invalid && std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {
+            invalid = std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR;
+        } else if (!invalid) {
+            invalid = std::abs((res - ref) / ref) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR;
+        }
+        if (invalid) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr,
+                         "Rank %d validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, "
+                         "got %.10e\n",
+                         rank, globalRowOffset + i, ref, res);
+            return false;
         }
     }
     return true;
 }

The final four-GPU run passed validation and preserved the one-rank output hash; a larger 4096² case also passed at roughly 282 GFLOP/s over four GPUs in this environment. The CUDA sanitizer installation is incomplete here (its injection library is missing), so validation relied on clean builds plus single-rank, multi-rank, sparse-edge, and larger numerical runs. The temporary build directory has been removed, leaving only the requested existing files modified.

    18	constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;

    21	// All CUDA failures are fatal: this benchmark intentionally has no CPU fallback.
    22	#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                  \
    23	    do {                                                                                  \
    24	        const cudaError_t error_ = (call);                                                 \
    25	        if (error_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                       \
    26	            std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d: %s\n", __FILE__, __LINE__,        \
    27	                         cudaGetErrorString(error_));                                      \
    28	            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);                                       \
    29	        }                                                                                 \
    30	    } while (false)

    32	void fill(double* values, const index_t count, const double maxValue) {
    33	    for (index_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
    34	        values[i] = maxValue * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));
    35	    }
    36	}
    37	
    38	// Keep the original deterministic CSR construction so the generated problem and
    39	// externally printed results retain the baseline benchmark's semantics.
    40	void initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t nnz,
    41	                      const index_t dim) {
    42	    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;
    43	    const double probability = static_cast<double>(nnz) /
    44	                               (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));
    45	
    46	    srand(8675309);
    47	    bool fillRemaining = false;
    48	    for (index_t row = 0; row < dim; ++row) {
    49	        rowDelimiters[row] = nnzAssigned;
    50	        for (index_t col = 0; col < dim; ++col) {
    51	            const uint64_t position = static_cast<uint64_t>(row) * dim + col;
    52	            const uint64_t entriesLeft = static_cast<uint64_t>(dim) * dim - position;
    53	            const index_t needToAssign = nnz - nnzAssigned;
    54	            if (entriesLeft <= needToAssign) {
    55	                fillRemaining = true;
    56	            }
    57	            const double randomValue = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;
    58	            if ((nnzAssigned < nnz && randomValue <= probability) || fillRemaining) {
    59	                cols[nnzAssigned++] = col;
    60	            }
    61	        }
    62	    }
    63	    rowDelimiters[dim] = nnz;
    64	}
    65	
    66	void spmvCpu(const double* values, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,
    67	             const double* vector, const index_t rows, double* output) {
    68	#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    69	    for (int64_t row = 0; row < static_cast<int64_t>(rows); ++row) {
    70	        double sum = 0.0;
    71	        for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row]; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1]; ++item) {
    72	            sum += values[item] * vector[cols[item]];
    73	        }
    74	        output[row] = sum;
    75	    }
    76	}
    77	
    78	// One thread per row is more efficient for very short rows because it avoids
    79	// spending an entire warp on only a handful of nonzeros.
    80	__global__ void spmvScalarKernel(const double* __restrict__ values,
    81	                                 const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
    82	                                 const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
    83	                                 const double* __restrict__ vector,
    84	                                 const index_t rows, double* __restrict__ output) {
    85	    const index_t row = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    86	    if (row >= rows) {
    87	        return;
    88	    }
    89	
    90	    double sum = 0.0;
    91	    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row]; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1]; ++item) {
    92	        sum = fma(values[item], vector[cols[item]], sum);
    93	    }
    94	    output[row] = sum;
    95	}
    96	
    97	// A warp cooperatively processes one row. CSR values and column indices are read
    98	// coalescently, while read-only vector entries benefit from the GPU cache.
    99	__global__ void spmvWarpKernel(const double* __restrict__ values,
   100	                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
   101	                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
   102	                               const double* __restrict__ vector,
   103	                               const index_t rows, double* __restrict__ output) {
   104	    const index_t globalThread = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
   105	    const index_t row = globalThread >> 5;
   106	    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & 31U;
   107	    if (row >= rows) {
   108	        return;
   109	    }
   110	
   111	    double sum = 0.0;
   112	    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row] + lane; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1];
   113	         item += 32) {
   114	        sum = fma(values[item], vector[cols[item]], sum);
   115	    }
   116	
   117	#pragma unroll
   118	    for (int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {
   119	        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffU, sum, offset);
   120	    }
   121	    if (lane == 0) {
   122	        output[row] = sum;
   123	    }
   124	}
   125	
   126	void launchSpmv(cudaStream_t stream, const double* values, const index_t* cols,
   127	                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vector, const index_t rows,
   128	                const index_t nnz, double* output) {
   129	    if (rows == 0) {
   130	        return;
   131	    }
   132	
   133	    const double averageNnz = static_cast<double>(nnz) / rows;
   134	    if (averageNnz < 12.0) {
   135	        const int blocks = static_cast<int>((rows + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE);
   136	        spmvScalarKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
   137	            values, cols, rowDelimiters, vector, rows, output);
   138	    } else {
   139	        constexpr int warpsPerBlock = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / 32;
   140	        const int blocks = static_cast<int>((rows + warpsPerBlock - 1) / warpsPerBlock);
   141	        spmvWarpKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
   142	            values, cols, rowDelimiters, vector, rows, output);
   143	    }
   144	}

   269	    const index_t globalRowBegin = static_cast<index_t>(

   273	    const index_t localRows = globalRowEnd - globalRowBegin;
   274	
   275	    std::vector<int> rowCounts(worldSize);
   276	    std::vector<int> rowDisplacements(worldSize);

   278	    std::vector<int> nnzCounts(worldSize);

   280	    for (int process = 0; process < worldSize; ++process) {

   283	        rowCounts[process] = static_cast<int>(end - begin);
   284	        rowDisplacements[process] = static_cast<int>(begin);
   285	        // Send each global row delimiter exactly once. The receiving rank adds

   289	
   290	    std::vector<double> globalValues;
   291	    std::vector<index_t> globalCols;
   292	    std::vector<index_t> globalRowDelimiters;
   293	    std::vector<double> hostVector(numRows);
   294	
   295	    if (rank == 0) {
   296	        globalValues.resize(totalNnz);
   297	        globalCols.resize(totalNnz);
   298	        globalRowDelimiters.resize(numRows + 1);
   299	
   300	        // The C standard specifies the initial rand state as if srand(1) had run.
   301	        // Make that baseline behavior explicit in the MPI program.
   302	        srand(1);
   303	        fill(hostVector.data(), numRows, maxValue);
   304	        fill(globalValues.data(), totalNnz, maxValue);
   305	        initRandomMatrix(globalCols.data(), globalRowDelimiters.data(), totalNnz, numRows);
   306	
   307	        for (int process = 0; process < worldSize; ++process) {
   308	            const int firstRow = rowDisplacements[process];
   309	            const int lastRow = firstRow + rowCounts[process];
   310	            nnzDisplacements[process] = static_cast<int>(globalRowDelimiters[firstRow]);
   311	            nnzCounts[process] = static_cast<int>(globalRowDelimiters[lastRow] -
   312	                                                  globalRowDelimiters[firstRow]);
   313	        }
   314	    }
   315	
   316	    MPI_Bcast(nnzCounts.data(), worldSize, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   317	    MPI_Bcast(nnzDisplacements.data(), worldSize, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   318	    MPI_Bcast(hostVector.data(), static_cast<int>(numRows), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   319	
   320	    const index_t localNnz = static_cast<index_t>(nnzCounts[rank]);
   321	    std::vector<double> hostValues(localNnz);
   322	    std::vector<index_t> hostCols(localNnz);
   323	    std::vector<index_t> hostRowDelimiters(localRows + 1);
   324	
   325	    MPI_Scatterv(rank == 0 ? globalValues.data() : nullptr, nnzCounts.data(),
   326	                 nnzDisplacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, hostValues.data(), nnzCounts[rank],
   327	                 MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   328	    MPI_Scatterv(rank == 0 ? globalCols.data() : nullptr, nnzCounts.data(),
   329	                 nnzDisplacements.data(), MPI_UINT32_T, hostCols.data(), nnzCounts[rank],
   330	                 MPI_UINT32_T, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   331	    MPI_Scatterv(rank == 0 ? globalRowDelimiters.data() : nullptr,
   332	                 delimiterCounts.data(), rowDisplacements.data(), MPI_UINT32_T,
   333	                 hostRowDelimiters.data(), static_cast<int>(localRows), MPI_UINT32_T, 0,
   334	                 MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   335	
   336	    const index_t localBase = hostRowDelimiters.front();
   337	    hostRowDelimiters[localRows] = localBase + localNnz;
   338	#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
   339	    for (int64_t row = 0; row <= static_cast<int64_t>(localRows); ++row) {
   340	        hostRowDelimiters[row] -= localBase;
   341	    }
   342	
   343	    // Release root-only global CSR storage before allocating device storage.
   344	    globalValues.clear();
   345	    globalValues.shrink_to_fit();
   346	    globalCols.clear();
   347	    globalCols.shrink_to_fit();
   348	    globalRowDelimiters.clear();
   349	    globalRowDelimiters.shrink_to_fit();
   350	
   351	    if (rank == 0) {
   352	        std::printf("Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\n");
   353	        std::printf("Matrix size: %u x %u\n", numRows, numRows);
   354	        std::printf("Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\n", sparsity);
   355	        std::printf("Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\n", totalNnz,
   356	                    100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(totalNnz) / matrixEntries));
   357	        std::printf("Iterations: %u\n", iterations);
   358	        std::printf("Max value: %.2f\n", maxValue);
   359	        std::printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   360	        std::printf("Hybrid configuration: %d MPI rank(s), up to %d OpenMP thread(s) "
   361	                    "per rank, CUDA device %d on rank 0\n",
   362	                    worldSize, omp_get_max_threads(), device);
   363	        std::printf("Initializing data structures...\n");
   364	    }
   365	
   366	    double* deviceValues = nullptr;
   367	    index_t* deviceCols = nullptr;
   368	    index_t* deviceRowDelimiters = nullptr;
   369	    double* deviceVector = nullptr;
   370	    double* deviceOutput = nullptr;
   371	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&deviceValues, std::max<size_t>(1, localNnz) * sizeof(double)));
   372	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&deviceCols, std::max<size_t>(1, localNnz) * sizeof(index_t)));
   373	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&deviceRowDelimiters, (localRows + 1) * sizeof(index_t)));
   374	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&deviceVector, numRows * sizeof(double)));
   375	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&deviceOutput, localRows * sizeof(double)));
   376	
   377	    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
   378	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
   379	    if (localNnz != 0) {
   380	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceValues, hostValues.data(), localNnz * sizeof(double),
   381	                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
   382	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceCols, hostCols.data(), localNnz * sizeof(index_t),
   383	                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
   384	    }
   385	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceRowDelimiters, hostRowDelimiters.data(),
   386	                               (localRows + 1) * sizeof(index_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice,
   387	                               stream));
   388	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceVector, hostVector.data(), numRows * sizeof(double),
   389	                               cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
   390	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
   391	
   392	    std::vector<double> reference;
   393	    if (validate) {
   394	        if (rank == 0) {
   395	            std::printf("Computing reference solution...\n");
   396	        }
   397	        reference.resize(localRows);
   398	        spmvCpu(hostValues.data(), hostCols.data(), hostRowDelimiters.data(), hostVector.data(),
   399	                localRows, reference.data());
   400	    }
   401	
   402	    // Warm up the selected kernel, then capture all iterations into one CUDA graph.
   403	    // This removes per-iteration host launch latency without changing the work done.
   404	    launchSpmv(stream, deviceValues, deviceCols, deviceRowDelimiters, deviceVector, localRows,
   405	               localNnz, deviceOutput);
   406	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   407	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
   408	
   409	    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
   410	    cudaGraphExec_t graphExec = nullptr;
   411	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
   412	    for (index_t iteration = 0; iteration < iterations; ++iteration) {
   413	        launchSpmv(stream, deviceValues, deviceCols, deviceRowDelimiters, deviceVector,
   414	                   localRows, localNnz, deviceOutput);
   415	    }
   416	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
   417	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graphExec, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
   418	
   419	    if (rank == 0) {
   420	        std::printf("Computing SpMV...\n");
   421	    }
   422	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   423	    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
   424	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(graphExec, stream));
   425	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
   426	    const double localElapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   427	
   428	    double elapsed = 0.0;
   429	    MPI_Reduce(&localElapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   430	
   431	    std::vector<double> localOutput;
   432	    if (validate || printResults) {
   433	        localOutput.resize(localRows);
   434	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(localOutput.data(), deviceOutput, localRows * sizeof(double),
   435	                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
   436	    }
   437	
   438	    int globallyValid = 1;
   439	    if (validate) {
   440	        const int locallyValid = verifyResults(reference.data(), localOutput.data(), localRows,
   441	                                               globalRowBegin, rank)
   442	                                     ? 1
   443	                                     : 0;
   444	        MPI_Allreduce(&locallyValid, &globallyValid, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   445	    }
   446	
   447	    std::vector<double> globalOutput;
   448	    if (printResults) {
   449	        if (rank == 0) {
   450	            globalOutput.resize(numRows);
   451	        }
   452	        MPI_Gatherv(localOutput.data(), static_cast<int>(localRows), MPI_DOUBLE,
   453	                    rank == 0 ? globalOutput.data() : nullptr, rowCounts.data(),
   454	                    rowDisplacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   455	    }
   456	
   457	    if (rank == 0) {
   458	        const double elapsedMs = elapsed * 1000.0;
   459	        const double averageMs = elapsedMs / iterations;
   460	        const double gflops = (2.0 * totalNnz * iterations) / elapsed / 1.0e9;
   461	        std::printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", elapsedMs);
   462	        std::printf("Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\n", averageMs);
   463	        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
   464	        if (printResults) {
   465	            print_results(globalOutput, "OutputVector");
   466	        }
   467	        if (validate) {
   468	            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
   469	            std::printf("Validation: %s\n", globallyValid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
   470	        }
   471	    }
   472	
   473	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphExecDestroy(graphExec));
   474	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphDestroy(graph));
   475	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(stream));
   476	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceOutput));
   477	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceVector));
   478	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceRowDelimiters));
   479	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceCols));
   480	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceValues));
   481	    MPI_Comm_free(&localComm);
   482	    MPI_Finalize();
   483	    return globallyValid ? EXIT_SUCCESS : EXIT_FAILURE;
   484	}
     1	cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.24)
     2	project(spmv_benchmark LANGUAGES CXX CUDA)

    10	endif()

    28	)

+void spmvCpu(const double* values, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,

+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)

-// ****************************************************************************
-// Function: verifyResults
-//
-// Purpose:
-//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution
-//
-// Arguments:
-//   reference: array holding the reference result vector
-//   result: array holding the result vector to verify
-//   size: number of elements per vector
-//
-// ****************************************************************************
-bool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {
+// One thread per row is more efficient for very short rows because it avoids
+// spending an entire warp on only a handful of nonzeros.
+__global__ void spmvScalarKernel(const double* __restrict__ values,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                 const double* __restrict__ vector,
+                                 const index_t rows, double* __restrict__ output) {
+    const index_t row = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row]; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1]; ++item) {
+        sum = fma(values[item], vector[cols[item]], sum);
+    }
+    output[row] = sum;
+}
+
+// A warp cooperatively processes one row. CSR values and column indices are read
+// coalescently, while read-only vector entries benefit from the GPU cache.
+__global__ void spmvWarpKernel(const double* __restrict__ values,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vector,
+                               const index_t rows, double* __restrict__ output) {
+    const index_t globalThread = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const index_t row = globalThread >> 5;
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & 31U;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row] + lane; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+         item += 32) {
+        sum = fma(values[item], vector[cols[item]], sum);
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffU, sum, offset);
+    }
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        output[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(cudaStream_t stream, const double* values, const index_t* cols,
+                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vector, const index_t rows,
+                const index_t nnz, double* output) {
+    if (rows == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const double averageNnz = static_cast<double>(nnz) / rows;
+    if (averageNnz < 12.0) {
+        const int blocks = static_cast<int>((rows + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE);
+        spmvScalarKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
+            values, cols, rowDelimiters, vector, rows, output);
+    } else {
+        constexpr int warpsPerBlock = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / 32;
+        const int blocks = static_cast<int>((rows + warpsPerBlock - 1) / warpsPerBlock);
+        spmvWarpKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
+            values, cols, rowDelimiters, vector, rows, output);
+    }
+}
+
+bool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size,
+                   const index_t globalRowOffset, const int rank) {
     for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {
         const double ref = reference[i];
         const double res = result[i];
-        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {
-            // For very small values, check absolute error

codex
Implemented the unconditional hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA SpMV benchmark.

Key changes:

- MPI row-wise CSR distribution with one local GPU per MPI rank.
- Adaptive CUDA scalar/warp kernels and CUDA Graph execution.
- OpenMP host-side CSR preparation and parallel CPU validation.
- Slowest-rank timing, distributed validation, and result gathering.
- Required MPI/OpenMP/CUDA configuration while preserving executable name `spmv`.

Files changed:

- [spmv.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/spmv_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_hybrid_r2/spmv/spmv.cpp:80)
- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/spmv_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_hybrid_r2/spmv/CMakeLists.txt:1)

Validation completed:

- Clean CUDA/MPI/OpenMP build.
- Single-rank and four-rank results produced identical hashes.
- Numerical validation passed for normal, empty-CSR, and 4096² cases.
- Four RTX 3090 run reached approximately 282 GFLOP/s on the 4096² test.
- Only the two existing source files remain; temporary build artifacts were removed.

+void spmvCpu(const double* values, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,

+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (int64_t row = 0; row < static_cast<int64_t>(rows); ++row) {
+        double sum = 0.0;

-// ****************************************************************************
-// Function: verifyResults
-//
-// Purpose:
-//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution
-//
-// Arguments:
-//   reference: array holding the reference result vector
-//   result: array holding the result vector to verify
-//   size: number of elements per vector
-//
-// ****************************************************************************
-bool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {
+// One thread per row is more efficient for very short rows because it avoids
+// spending an entire warp on only a handful of nonzeros.
+__global__ void spmvScalarKernel(const double* __restrict__ values,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                 const double* __restrict__ vector,
+                                 const index_t rows, double* __restrict__ output) {
+    const index_t row = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row]; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1]; ++item) {
+        sum = fma(values[item], vector[cols[item]], sum);
+    }
+    output[row] = sum;
+}
+
+// A warp cooperatively processes one row. CSR values and column indices are read
+// coalescently, while read-only vector entries benefit from the GPU cache.
+__global__ void spmvWarpKernel(const double* __restrict__ values,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vector,
+                               const index_t rows, double* __restrict__ output) {
+    const index_t globalThread = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const index_t row = globalThread >> 5;
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & 31U;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (index_t item = rowDelimiters[row] + lane; item < rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+         item += 32) {
+        sum = fma(values[item], vector[cols[item]], sum);
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffU, sum, offset);
+    }
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        output[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(cudaStream_t stream, const double* values, const index_t* cols,
+                const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vector, const index_t rows,
+                const index_t nnz, double* output) {
+    if (rows == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const double averageNnz = static_cast<double>(nnz) / rows;
+    if (averageNnz < 12.0) {
+        const int blocks = static_cast<int>((rows + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE);
+        spmvScalarKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
+            values, cols, rowDelimiters, vector, rows, output);
+    } else {
+        constexpr int warpsPerBlock = CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE / 32;
+        const int blocks = static_cast<int>((rows + warpsPerBlock - 1) / warpsPerBlock);
+        spmvWarpKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(
+            values, cols, rowDelimiters, vector, rows, output);
+    }
+}
+
+bool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size,
+                   const index_t globalRowOffset, const int rank) {
     for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {
         const double ref = reference[i];
         const double res = result[i];
-        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {
-            // For very small values, check absolute error
-            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
-                printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\n", i, ref, res);
-                return false;
-            }
-        } else {
-            // Check relative error
-            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);
-            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
-                printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\n",
-                       i, ref, res, relError);
-                return false;
-            }
+        bool invalid = !std::isfinite(ref) || !std::isfinite(res);
+        if (!invalid && std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {
+            invalid = std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR;
+        } else if (!invalid) {
+            invalid = std::abs((res - ref) / ref) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR;
+        }
+        if (invalid) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr,
+                         "Rank %d validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, "
+                         "got %.10e\n",
+                         rank, globalRowOffset + i, ref, res);
+            return false;
         }
     }
     return true;
 }

Implemented the unconditional hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA SpMV benchmark.

Key changes:

- MPI row-wise CSR distribution with one local GPU per MPI rank.
- Adaptive CUDA scalar/warp kernels and CUDA Graph execution.
- OpenMP host-side CSR preparation and parallel CPU validation.
- Slowest-rank timing, distributed validation, and result gathering.
- Required MPI/OpenMP/CUDA configuration while preserving executable name `spmv`.
